<script id="rc-qrcode-script">
(function(){
  function createReceiptAuth(){
    const codeEl = document.getElementById('receiptAuthCode');
    const qrEl = document.getElementById('receiptQrCode');
    if(!codeEl || !qrEl || typeof QRCode === 'undefined') return;

    const stamp = new Date();
    const code = 'RC-' +
      stamp.getFullYear() + '-' +
      String(stamp.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') +
      String(stamp.getDate()).padStart(2,'0') + '-' +
      Math.floor(Math.random()*9000+1000);

    codeEl.textContent = code;

    qrEl.innerHTML = '';

    new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code + '|Emitido em ' + stamp.toLocaleString('pt-BR'),
      width:76,
      height:76
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', createReceiptAuth);
  }else{
    createReceiptAuth();
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="rc-qrcode-force-visible">
(function(){
function buildQr(){
  const codeEl=document.getElementById('receiptAuthCodeVisible');
  const qrEl=document.getElementById('receiptQrCodeVisible');

  if(!codeEl || !qrEl) return;

  const now=new Date();
  const code='RC-'+now.getFullYear()+'-'+String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+
  String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*90000+10000);

  codeEl.textContent=code;

  qrEl.innerHTML='';

  if(typeof QRCode!=='undefined'){
      new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code,
      width:94,
      height:94
    });
  }else{
    qrEl.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-align:center;color:#0F2A3A">QR indisponível</div>';
  }
}
window.addEventListener('load',buildQr);
setTimeout(buildQr,1200);
})();
</script>

const db=new Dexie('ReciboCondoPrestadoresDB');
db.version(1).stores({providers:'++id,name,category,document',services:'++id,providerId,category,reference,serviceDate,dueDate,status',receipts:'++id,number,date,providerName,category',settings:'key',meta:'key'});
db.version(2).stores({providers:'++id,name,category,document',services:'++id,providerId,category,reference,serviceDate,dueDate,status,recurrenceId',receipts:'++id,number,date,providerName,category',settings:'key',meta:'key',recurrences:'++id,providerId,category,active'});
db.version(3).stores({providers:'++id,name,category,document',services:'++id,providerId,category,reference,serviceDate,dueDate,status,recurrenceId',receipts:'++id,number,date,providerName,category',settings:'key',meta:'key',recurrences:'++id,providerId,category,active',logs:'++id,at,type,title,detail,role'});
function app(){return{

  categories:['Jardineiro','Vidraceiro','Serralheiro','Eletricista','Pedreiro','Paisagista','Pintor','Encanador','Limpeza','Dedetização','Manutenção de bomba','Portão/Interfone','Gratificação natalina','Outros'],
  providers:[],services:[],receipts:[],recurrences:[],logs:[],receiptSeqValue:0,receiptSeqInput:"",settings:{condoName:'Condomínio Exemplo',cnpj:'CNPJ 00.000.000/0001-00',address:'Endereço do condomínio',manager:'Síndico/Tesoureiro',logo:''},
  providerForm:{id:null,name:'',type:'Autônomo',document:'',category:'Jardineiro',phone:'',whatsapp:'',pix:'',notes:''},providerSearch:'',providerCategoryFilter:'',providerSortField:'name',providerSortDir:'asc',
  serviceForm:{providerId:'',category:'Jardineiro',reference:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,7),serviceDate:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),dueDate:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),amount:0,location:'',description:'',paymentDate:'',paymentMethod:'',paidValue:0},

  serviceFilter:'todos',serviceCategoryFilter:'todas',serviceProviderFilter:'todos',serviceMonthFilter:'',paymentMonthFilter:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,7),paymentStatusFilter:'todos',paymentProviderFilter:'todos',receiptFilter:{q:'',month:'',cat

ooltipTag:'Recibos '+receiptsMonth,tooltip:['Valor médio por pagamento: '+this.money(countMonth?paidMonth/countMonth:0)+'.', 'Recibos emitidos no mês: '+receiptsMonth+'.', 'Bom para conferir se todos os pagamentos pagos já têm recibo.']},
    {label:'Prestadores ativos',value:this.providers.length,hint:'cadastrados',icon:'users',color:'text-rc-600',tooltipTitle:'Base de prestadores',tooltipMain:this.providers.length+' cadastro(s)',tooltipTag:'Cadastros',tooltip:['Categorias em uso: '+(activeCats.length?activeCats.join(', '):'nenhuma categoria cadastrada.'), 'Use para localizar quem presta serviço recorrente ou avulso.', 'Dica: mantenha PIX, WhatsApp e categoria sempre atualizados.']},
    {label:'Em aberto no ano',value:this.money(open),hint:'pendente/parcial',icon:'triangle-alert',color:'text-red-600',tooltipTitle:'Pendências do ano',tooltipMain:this.money(open),tooltipTag:'Atenção',tooltip:['Saldo lançado e ainda não quitado no ano.', 'Maiores pendências: '+(openTop.length?openTop.join(' | '):'nenhuma pendência no ano.'), 'Depois de pagar, registre o pagamento antes de emitir o recibo.']},
    {label:'Maior categoria',value:top?top.category:'-',hint:top?this.money(top.total):'sem dados',icon:'trophy',color:'text-yellow-600',tooltipTitle:'Categoria líder',tooltipMain:top?top.category:'Sem dados',tooltipTag:top?this.money(top.total):'Sem dados',tooltip:['Categoria com maior volume pago no ano.', 'Ranking: '+(catTop.length?catTop.join(' | '):'sem categorias pagas no ano.'), 'Ajuda a identificar onde o condomínio mais gastou.']}
  ]},
  get annualTotals(){let paid=this.yearlyRows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0),open=this.yearlyRows.reduce((a,s)=>a+this.balance(s),0),count=this.yearlyRows.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0).length;return{paid,open,count}},
  get monthlyHistory(){let labels=['Janeiro','Fevereiro','Março','Abril','Maio','Junho','Julho','Agosto','Setembro','Outubro','Novembro','Dezembro'];return labels.map((label,i)=>{let key=String(this.dashboardYear)+'-'+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'), rows=this.yearlyRows.filter(s=>String(s.paymentDate||s.serviceDate||'').slice(0,7)===key), paid=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0), open=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+this.balance(s),0), map={};rows.forEach(s=>{map[s.category]=(map[s.category]||0)+Number(s.paidValue||s.amount||0)});let top=Object.entries(map).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0];return{key,label,count:rows.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0).length,paid,open,topCategory:top?top[0]:'-'}})},
  get annualTopProviders(){let map={};this.yearlyRows.forEach(s=>{let id=s.providerId,p=this.providers.find(x=>Number(x.id)===Number(id))||{};if(!map[id])map[id]={id,name:p.name||'-',category:p.category||s.category,count:0,total:0,open:0};if(Number(s.paidValue||0)>0)map[id].count++;map[id].total+=Number(s.paidValue||0);map[id].open+=this.balance(s)});return Object.values(map).filter(x=>x.total>0||x.open>0).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total).slice(0,10)},
  get annualCategorySummary(){let rows=this.yearlyRows,total=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0)||1;return this.categories.map(category=>{let val=rows.filter(s=>s.category===category).reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0);return{category,total:val,percent:Math.min(100,Math.round(val/total*100))}}).filter(x=>x.total>0).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total)},
  get topProviders(){return this.annualTopProviders},
  get categorySummary(){return this.annualCategorySummary},
  get openServices(){return this.services.filter(s=>s.status!=='Pago').sort((a,b)=>String(a.dueDate).localeCompare(String(b.dueDate)))},
  get filteredProviders(){
    let q=(this.q||'').toLowerCase();
    let ps=(this.providerSearch||'').toLowerCase();
    let cat=this.providerCategoryFilter||'';
    let list=this.providers.filter(p=>{
      if(q&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))return false;
      if(ps&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(ps))return false;
      if(cat&&p.category!==cat)return false;
      return true;
    });
    let field=this.providerSortField||'name';
    let dir=this.providerSortDir||'asc';
    list=[...list].sort((a,b)=>{
      let va=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(a.id):String(a[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      let vb=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(b.id):String(b[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      if(va<vb)return dir==='asc'?-1:1;
      if(va>vb)return dir==='asc'?1:-1;
      return 0;
    });
    return list;
  },
  get filteredServices(){return this.services.filter(s=>(this.serviceFilter==='todos'||s.status===this.serviceFilter)&&(this.serviceCategoryFilter==='todas'||s.category===this.serviceCategoryFilter)&&(this.serviceProviderFilter==='todos'||Number(s.providerId)===Number(this.serviceProviderFilter))&&(!this.serviceMonthFilter||s.reference===this.serviceMonthFilter)).sort((a,b)=>String(b.serviceDate).localeCompare(String(a.serviceDate)))},
  get filteredPayments(){return this.services.filter(s=>(!this.paymentMonthFilter||s.reference===this.paymentMonthFilter)&&(this.paymentStatusFilter==='todos'||s.status===this.paymentStatusFilter)&&(this.paymentProviderFilter==='todos'||Number(s.providerId)===Number(this.paymentProviderFilter))).sort((a,b)=>String(a.dueDate||'9999-12-31').localeCompare(String(b.dueDate||'9999-12-31')))},
  get paymentSummary(){let rows=this.filteredPayments, paid=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0), expected=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.amount||0),0), open=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+this.balance(s),0), paidCount=rows.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0).length, missingReceipt=rows.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0&&this.reportReceiptNumber(s)==='-').length;return{rows,expected,paid,open,paidCount,missingReceipt,overdue:rows.filter(s=>s.status!=='Pago'&&s.dueDate<this.today()).length,today:rows.filter(s=>s.status!=='Pago'&&s.dueDate===this.today()).length}},
  get paymentRecent(){return this.services.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0).sort((a,b)=>String(b.paymentDate||'').localeCompare(String(a.paymentDate||''))).slice(0,8)},
  get servicePanelRows(){return this.services.filter(s=>s.reference===this.servicePanelMonth).sort((a,b)=>String(b.paymentDate||b.serviceDate).localeCompare(String(a.paymentDate||a.serviceDate)))},
  get servicePanelPaidRows(){return this.servicePanelRows.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0).sort((a,b)=>String(b.paymentDate||b.serviceDate).localeCompare(String(a.paymentDate||a.serviceDate)))},
  get servicePanelOpenRows(){return this.servicePanelRows.filter(s=>this.balance(s)>0).sort((a,b)=>String(a.dueDate||'2999-12-31').localeCompare(String(b.dueDate||'2999-12-31')))},
  get servicePanelTotals(){let expected=this.servicePanelRows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.amount||0),0),paid=this.servicePanelRows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0);return{expected,paid,open:Math.max(0,expected-paid)}},
  get servicePanelRecurringGenerated(){return this.recurrences.filter(r=>r.active&&this.recurrenceGenerated(r,this.servicePanelMonth)

<script id="rc-qrcode-script">
(function(){
  function createReceiptAuth(){
    const codeEl = document.getElementById('receiptAuthCode');
    const qrEl = document.getElementById('receiptQrCode');
    if(!codeEl || !qrEl || typeof QRCode === 'undefined') return;

    const stamp = new Date();
    const code = 'RC-' +
      stamp.getFullYear() + '-' +
      String(stamp.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') +
      String(stamp.getDate()).padStart(2,'0') + '-' +
      Math.floor(Math.random()*9000+1000);

    codeEl.textContent = code;

    qrEl.innerHTML = '';

    new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code + '|Emitido em ' + stamp.toLocaleString('pt-BR'),
      width:76,
      height:76
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', createReceiptAuth);
  }else{
    createReceiptAuth();
  }
})();
</script>


<script id="rc-qrcode-force-visible">
(function(){
function buildQr(){
  const codeEl=document.getElementById('receiptAuthCodeVisible');
  const qrEl=document.getElementById('receiptQrCodeVisible');

  if(!codeEl || !qrEl) return;

  const now=new Date();
  const code='RC-'+now.getFullYear()+'-'+String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+
  String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*90000+10000);

  codeEl.textContent=code;

  qrEl.innerHTML='';

  if(typeof QRCode!=='undefined'){
      new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code,
      width:94,
      height:94
    });
  }else{
    qrEl.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-align:center;color:#0F2A3A">QR indisponível</div>';
  }
}
window.addEventListener('load',buildQr);
setTimeout(buildQr,1200);
})();
</script>


<script id="rc-report-logo-sync">
window.addEventListener('load',function(){
  try{
    const existingLogo=document.querySelector('.rc-icon,.receipt-logo img,.app-logo img,img');
    const reportLogo=document.getElementById('rcReportLogo');

    if(existingLogo && reportLogo && existingLogo.src){
      reportLogo.src=existingLogo.src;
    }
  }catch(e){}
});
</script>

</body>

<script id="rc-qrcode-script">
(function(){
  function createReceiptAuth(){
    const codeEl = document.getElementById('receiptAuthCode');
    const qrEl = document.getElementById('receiptQrCode');
    if(!codeEl || !qrEl || typeof QRCode === 'undefined') return;

    const stamp = new Date();
    const code = 'RC-' +
      stamp.getFullYear() + '-' +
      String(stamp.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') +
      String(stamp.getDate()).padStart(2,'0') + '-' +
      Math.floor(Math.random()*9000+1000);

    codeEl.textContent = code;

    qrEl.innerHTML = '';

    new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code + '|Emitido em ' + stamp.toLocaleString('pt-BR'),
      width:76,
      height:76
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', createReceiptAuth);
  }else{
    createReceiptAuth();
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="rc-qrcode-script">
(function(){
  function createReceiptAuth(){
    const codeEl = document.getElementById('receiptAuthCode');
    const qrEl = document.getElementById('receiptQrCode');
    if(!codeEl || !qrEl || typeof QRCode === 'undefined') return;

    const stamp = new Date();
    const code = 'RC-' +
      stamp.getFullYear() + '-' +
      String(stamp.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') +
      String(stamp.getDate()).padStart(2,'0') + '-' +
      Math.floor(Math.random()*9000+1000);

    codeEl.textContent = code;

    qrEl.innerHTML = '';

    new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code + '|Emitido em ' + stamp.toLocaleString('pt-BR'),
      width:76,
      height:76
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', createReceiptAuth);
  }else{
    createReceiptAuth();
  }
})();
</script>


<script id="rc-qrcode-force-visible">
(function(){
function buildQr(){
  const codeEl=document.getElementById('receiptAuthCodeVisible');
  const qrEl=document.getElementById('receiptQrCodeVisible');

  if(!codeEl || !qrEl) return;

  const now=new Date();
  const code='RC-'+now.getFullYear()+'-'+String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+
  String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*90000+10000);

  codeEl.textContent=code;

  qrEl.innerHTML='';

  if(typeof QRCode!=='undefined'){
      new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code,
      width:94,
      height:94
    });
  }else{
    qrEl.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-align:center;color:#0F2A3A">QR indisponível</div>';
  }
}
window.addEventListener('load',buildQr);
setTimeout(buildQr,1200);
})();
</script>
